$(window).resize(function()
{
	dismiss_search_results();
});

$('body :not(.game_selector)').bind('click touch', function(e)
{
	if($('.game_selector').css('display')=='block')
	{
		e.stopPropagation();
				if(($(this).attr('class') || $(this).attr('id')) && $(this).attr('class')!='game_selector' && $(this).attr('id')!='game_search' && $(this).attr('id')!='searchtextbox' && $(this).attr('class')!='head')
		{
			dismiss_search_results();
		}
			}
});

$('#searchtextbox').autocomplete(
{
	minLength: 1,
	delay: 250,
	classes: { 'ui-autocomplete':'game_selector' },
	source: '/ajax/home_game_search?term=' + $('#searchtextbox').val(),
	focus: function(){ return false; },
	close: function(event, ui)
	{
		if($('#searchtextbox').val())
		{
			$('.game_selector').show();
			if($('.game_selector').has('li').length===0)
				dismiss_search_results();
		}
	},
	select: function(event, ui)
	{
		log_site_result(ui.item.row_num, 'game_faq_text');
		if(event.keyCode!=13)
			event.stopPropagation();
		else
			window.location = ui.item.url;
	}
})
.data('ui-autocomplete')._renderItem = function(ul, item)
{
	var list;
	if(item.product)
	{
		var contribs = '';
		contribs += 'Data&nbsp;&nbsp;';
		if(item.has_guides>0)
			contribs += 'Guides&nbsp;&nbsp;';
		if(item.has_cheats>0)
			contribs += 'Cheats&nbsp;&nbsp;';
		contribs += 'Q&A&nbsp;&nbsp;';
		contribs += 'Board';
		if(item.pcnt>5)
			item.plats = item.pcnt + ' platforms';

		list += ''+item.game_name+''+contribs+''+item.release_date+''+item.plats+'';
	}
	else if(item.footer)
	{
		list += ''+item.text+'';
	}
	else
	{
		list += 'No Results Found - Close';
	}
	return $(list).appendTo(ul);
};

		Guide and Walkthrough (DS) by Genroh
		
						Version: 2.5 | Updated: 08/07/2007															





	******************************************************************************
******************************************************************************
                             JUMP! ULTIMATE STARS
******************************************************************************
******************************************************************************

JUMP! ULTIMATE STARS
  System: Nintendo DS
  Developer: Ganbarion 
  Publisher: Nintendo & Shueisha Publishing (Shounen Jump)
  Genre: Action/Fighting Game

  Author: Genroh
  Version: 2.5
  Last Update: 08/07/07

(NOTE: Best viewed in "Courier New" font. If you're having trouble finding 
a section or character, use the ctrl + F command on your keyboard to find 
What you're looking for.)

============================================================================
UPDATES
============================================================================

11/25/06 - Walkthrough complete. Big thanks to Tilak for help in confirming 
The "Never use Block" mission objective. (Yes I'm aware some are still having 
Problems with missions with this requirement, I'll have it fixed shortly)

11/27/06 - Training and J-Arena menus translated. (Sorry for the wait on those)
New secret in the secrets section.
Finally got the mission that one requirement (Thanks to Muka at gameFAQs)

11/28/06 - Help Koma system is explained and almost all have been identified.
(Huge thanks to confucious for helping with this)

11/30/06 - Walkthrough corrections made.

12/01/06 - Wireless and Wifi menus mostly translated.

12/04/06 - Character section added, Items & Status effects added.

12/13/06 - Deck Maker menus finished, Passive effects in the works for 
all Battle characters. (Thanks to TatsuYagyu)

12/16/06 - Corrections and some other things.

12/27/06 - Well after cramming and cramming, finally got almost all 
the Passive effect translated. You'll also find a new Pre-made Deck page.
Credit to Shiryu EXE for that.

12/29/06 - World unlockers added to the Secrets section. 

01/02/07 - Deck Power!! Find out what it means in the Status effects 
section. More Pre-made deck added, and a new trick added for Killua.

01/30/07 - EVOLUTION CHARTS COMPLETE!! Sorry for the wait. More info added 
to koma evolution explanations, spelling and gramer errors corrected 
(some anyway). And the "Pink" gems are renamed "Purple".

8/07/07 - Contrary to popular belief I'm not dead nor have I lost interest.
          I've just been busy and my Wifi router is broken. :P Any way here's 
          a small update to appease you. Errors have been fixed and sorted. 
          If I missed any, please email me and point them out. 
          Currently working on revamping the walkthrough and adding some attack 
          descriptions and getting Mission Try mode it's section done. 
          If you want to contribute strategies for the revamped walkthrough 
          please note my email in the Legal section and send them in.


============================================================================
INTRODUCTION
============================================================================

Back in August last year a game was released on the DS that brought new 
meaning to hand-held fighting games. Well a little over one year later, 
the same people that brought you Jump Super Stars comes, Jump Ultimate Stars!
And as with last year, several great translators, gamers, and I have 
compiled another guide for the use of you the fans. The goal of this guide 
is to help those not fluent in Japanese or who are just stuck and need a 
little help, complete the game, unlock everything and enjoy the cool new 
Wifi features with your friends over the net. Now on with the guide. ^_^

Bear in mind that this is still in the works, and will be finished in due time.

============================================================================
INDEX
============================================================================

C1. Menu Navigation
    - J-Universe
    - J-Arena
    - Wireless
    - J-Quiz
    - Deck Maker
    - J-Power
    - Data
    - Options
C2. How to Play
    - In-Game Controls
    - The Gauges
    - Battle System
    - Hot-Key & Leader System
    - Items & Status Effects
    - Ultimate Actions
    - Dream Combo
C3. All About Koma
    - Koma Nature
    - Koma Evolution
    - Help Character
    - Support Character
    - Battle Character
C4. J-Universe Walkthrough
C5. Koma (Character) List
C6. Secrets
C7. Pre-Made Decks
C8. Legal Jargon
C9. Credits

*For faster navigation, use the ctrl + F command, type in the key words 
and begin your search. 

**Again this guide is best viewed in "Courier New" font.


============================================================================
C1: MENU NAVIGATION
============================================================================

Please take a moment to memorize the two buttons below, as they will allow 
you to select modes of play, options, and Koma (see below for details)
A = Confirm
B = Cancel
 ________       ________        ________        ________ 
|   J    |     |   J    |      |        |      |   J    |
|Universe|     | Arena  |      |Wireless|      |  Quiz  |
|________|     |________|      |________|      |________|

 ________       ________        ________        ________ 
|  Deck  |     |   J    |      |        |      |        |
|  Maker |     | Power  |      |  Data  |      | Option |
|________|     |________|      |________|      |________|


(NOTE: You can move the characters on the bottom around with your stylus)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
J-UNIVERSE
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

This is your basic story/adventure mode. Play through here to unlock 
characters, stages, menu characters, etc. etc.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
J-ARENA
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

-RANKING-
In Ranking mode you go up against various pre-made decks, 
as well as ones you've acquired in Wireless and Wifi play.

When you clear a match, you are prompted to choose to either;
Continue (continue through the ranking)
Save and Quit (you save your current rank)
Quit (no save)

When you want to continue, click on the Ranking you wish to 
continue on and choose either;

Continue | New | Cancel

 - Continue = From where you played last time with the same deck
 - New = Start a new Ranking
(NOTE: Selecting this will erase previous Ranking data of that match type)
 - Cancel = Go back to the Rank Match select screen

POINT MATCH RANKING
Play through 20 Ranking matches using the Point system.
(See Battle System in the How to Play section of the FAQ)

ELIMINATION MATCH RANKING
Play through 20 Ranking matches using the Elimination system.
(See Battle System in the How to Play section of the FAQ)

J-SYMBOL MATCH RANKING
Play through 20 Ranking matches using the J-Symbol system.
(See Battle System in the How to Play section of the FAQ)

CHAMPION MATCH RANKING
Play through 20 Ranking matches using a random combination of Point, 
Elimination and the J-Symbol system.
(unlocked after clearing Point, Elimination and J-Symbol ranking)

SCORE BOARD
View your scores in here. (Use L & R to scroll through)

-MISSION TRY-

After clearing certain missions ,you can come here to Mission Try mode
and replay them. Afterwards you are scored on your progress.
(best time, score, etc.)

The kanji on the side denotes whether it's scored based on time or points.

-BATTLE-
Go here to battle against AI opponents

DECK SELECT
- L & R = Scroll between decks you made, pre-made, and downloaded decks.
- Start button = Edit deck
- B button = Go back to previous menu

STAGE SELECT
You'll note the shapes by each stage, these tell you the type of stage 
it is for example.

[] = Compacted Stage
[   ] = Long horizontal stage (lots of dashing)
--
   = Long vertical stage (lots of jumping)
--

- Start button = Use the Direction pad and pan across the stage 
to see where things are. 
- Select button = Scroll through stages faster. (I think
- Y button = See where the Players spawn in the stage.
- B button = Go back to previous menu

BATTLE SETTINGS
   (Battle type)           (Timer)            (COM opponents)
(Item boxes On | Off) (Hazards On | Off) (Free-for-all | Team Battle)

- Battle Types = Choose between Point Match, Elimination and J-Symbol style 
matches.
(See Battle System in the How to Play section of the FAQ)

- Timer = You can set you timer between 30, 60 and 90 seconds long)

- COM Opponents = This is only available in Solo Battle and Training modes.
Basically you just select how many opponents you want (1, 2, or 3). You can 
also select whether you want the match to be Free-for-all or Team battle.
With Team battle once you've selected it, scroll down to the opponents 
deck you want on your team and press A (it should now change from team B 
to team A). 

- AI DIFFICULTY
To the right of the opponent decks, you can set the AI difficulty 
from easy, normal, hard.

OPPONENT DECKS
By scrolling down to your opponent’s deck, you can change their deck to 
another deck of your choice. Just go over to the desired opponent, press 
A and select a deck (just like you would for yourself).

PAUSE MENU
- Resume game (press Start)
- Reset
- Change Deck
- Main Menu

-TRAINING-
Go here to train your skills.

Preparation settings are the same as above however, Battle types 
and Timer adjust are disabled.

PAUSE MENU
- Resume game (press Start)
- COM settings : Stand, AI Controlled, Block, Jump
- Special Gauge recovery : ON, OFF
- Reset
- Change Deck
- Main Menu


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
WIRELESS
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

--WIRELESS PLAY--

DS WIRELESS BATTLE
(Play with 1-4 player Multi Cart battle)
 - Leader Member Select = Join an existing game or make make 
a new one with you as leader.

????? <-- (Not sure about this one. :P)

DOWNLOAD BATTLE

- Play with up to 4 players with only 1 cart. However, you and 
the other players will be given pre-made decks.

You will be given either of four Battle characters, 2 supports and 
several random Help characters.

Battle Characters
4 Koma Kinnikuman (Kinnikuman)
4 Koma Naruto Uzumaki (Naurto)
4 Koma Kenshiro (Hokuto no Ken)
4 Koma Monkey D. Luffy (One Piece)

Support Characters
2-3 Koma Gintoki Sakata (Gintama)
2-3 Koma Ryotsu Kankichi (KochiKame)
2-3 Koma Seiya (Saint Sieya)
2-3 Koma Son Goku (Dragon Ball)

DATA EXCHANGE

- Trade decks and Koma with other players.

QUIZ BATTLE

- Play a 2 player game of the JUS Quiz Mode 
(Both players will have to unlock this for it to be played)


--WIFI PLAY--

CONNECT TO WIFI

- Connect (Yes or No)

Battle Japan | Friend Battle
(tap X to enable Voice chat in Friend Battle for before and after battle)

 - Battle Japan = Go here to join random battles with random people.
 -- Start = Ready to fight
 -- Y = View the Match setup to help decide
 -- B = Cancel and leave
 - Friend Battle = Battle your friends over Wifi via friend codes.
 -- Leader Select = Join an existing room or become room leader

When you are Room Leader...
 - Select = Select a random Stage during Stage select 


WIFI FRIEND CODES
Friend Codes List | Rival Decks | Your Friend Code | Enter Friend code

 - Friend Codes List = View or delete your saved friend codes.
 - Rival Decks = View the decks of your past opponents.
 - Your Friend Code = ...does this really need explaining?
 - Enter Friend Code = Enter new codes here, they will be added 
to the Friend Codes section.


WIFI SETTINGS


--PASSWORD--
The password system is for exchanging decks without connecting directly 
and use a combination of the English alphabet, Arabic numbers, Japanese 
katakana/hiragana and random symbols.


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
J-QUIZ
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
(J-Quiz is unlocked once you clear the game)

You will recieve gems as a prize for answering questions correctly. 
You do not have to answer all of them correctly.

10 QUIZ QUESTION MODE
(Go through 10 quiz questions)

30 QUIZ QUESTION MODE
(Go through 30 quiz questions)

50 QUIZ QUESTION MODE
(Go through 50 quiz questions)

NOTE: There will be no translation for this section as the questions 
generate at random so there's really no point.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
DECK MAKER
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

-Select New to make new deck-

DECK EDITING
--Deck Edit
--Rename Deck
--Copy Deck 
(NOTE: Once you copy your deck, you'll have to rename the copy of the deck)
--Move Deck
--Delete Deck
--Cancel

MAKING A NEW DECK
--Make new
--Cancel
---L = Backspace
---R = Forwardspace
---B = Erase
---select = Change Font
---start = Done

NAVIGATING THE KOMA MENU
R button
--Default Koma Listing
--List Koma from A-Z
--List by Koma Number
--List in order of Battle, Support and Help
--List by Koma Nature (Power, Knowledge, Laughter and Null (Help komas))
--Cancel

L button (from left to right)
--List only Series Icon (Cancel at the bottom)
--List only their starting Hiragana (Cancel at the bottom)
A, Ka, Sa, Ta, Na
Ha, Ma, Ya, Ra, Wa
--List only Koma number (1 Help, 2-3 Support, and 4-8 Battle)
 (Cancel at the bottom)
--List only Koma Shape (Cancel at the bottom)
--List only Battle, Support or Help komas (Cancel at the bottom)
--List only Koma of a Specific Nature (Cancel at the bottom)
Power, Knowledge, Laughter, Null (Help Komas)
--Default Listing

 - Start = Training Mode (Test your new deck)
 -- Training Mode Pause menu
    - COM AI: Neutral, AI Control, Guard, Jump
    - Gauge Restore: ON | OFF
    - KOMAMAN Type: Power, Knowledge, Laughter
    - Return to Deck Maker
    - Reset Training

 - Select = Help (This will tell you what’s all on the menu)
(Note: this info is in Japanese)
 - Y = Tap Y a few times to see all of the Koma info 
(Character Page, Attack Info, Character Info, Boost Quotes, Koma picture)
 - X = Organize your Koma Deck/Page
 - Cancel
(Save Deck | Don't Save Deck | Cancel)

After you've named your deck, you can now add characters to it.
When you are done, press L or R and designate one of your Battle 
characters as the Leader (your deck won't save or be useable until 
you do this). You can also Hot-Key the L and R buttons to either 
another Battle character or a Support character.


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
J-POWER
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

This is where you gain new Koma. Don't let the term "Evolution" scare you. 
You're simply buying new komas, not replacing old ones.

First off, select a character. You should now see that character's 
Evolution Chart. 

    |-[?]
    |
[H]-|-[?]

^This is a standard evolution chart. They may vary depending on which 
character you pick. When you highlight a box, you will see how much it 
costs to buy it.

Here are some of the symbols you will run across in the Evolution charts.

H = Help koma (This is what you start with
S# = Support Koma # indicating size (They run out and attack)
B# = Battle Koma # indicating size (You control these characters)

Data = A blue box with a line and a dot.
(Unlocks either Manga info, Music track, or a World Demo)

Info Character = A purple little man with a word balloon.
(The Help Character this stems from can be set as your menu character 
via the Main Menu Options)

Evolution Path = A blue box with the following picture...
[]--[]
 |
 ---[]
(This unlocks an alternate koma path for a Battle character in the same series)

Stage = A red box with several little lines across it.
(Unlocks the stage of the respective series or one of the neutral stages 
for you to battle in)

Quiz = A green box with a "Q" in it.
(Unlocks a new set of quiz questions in Quiz mode.)

Worlds = A brown box with a picture of a galaxy in it.
(Unlocks new planets where you can gain new characters.)


Example chart:
    |-[S2]-[S3]
    |
[H]-|-[B4]-[B5]-|-[B6]
    |           |
    |-[E]-[D]   |-[AB6]

H = Help
S = Support
B = Battle
E = Evolution Path
D = Data
AB = Alternate Battle
C = Info Character
ST = Stage

^Depending on the character you are buying from, the top row is 
almost always Support koma, the middle row Battle koma, and bottom row 
alternate Battle koma (Same koma, different shape and nature) and unlockables.

There are however some characters that are Support or Help only and only 
have Support or Data, Evolution extension, etc. instead of Battle komas.


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
DATA
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Data options from left to right going down...

{Player Profile}  {Koma Gallery}
{Manga Gallery}   {Music Gallery}
{Movie Gallery}

-PLAYER PROFILE-
Name: (Your DS name here)
Location: (choose your location from a list, Japanese locations only) 
Comment: (Type something here for multiplayer)

Time Played: (Time you've spent playing the game)
Koma: (the total number of Koma you've unlocked, 870 is the max)
J-Gems: (shows how many of each gem you have)
Battle Record: ## Games Played | ## Linked Games Played | ## Linked Game Wins
Max Combo: ## | Max Damage: ###

-KOMA GALLERY-
View all of your komas you've unlocked.

-MANGA INFORMATION-
View the Volume 1 manga cover and a brief description of the manga.
You can unlock Manga Info via the koma evolution charts.

-MUSIC GALLERY-
Listen to music you unlocked. You can unlock Music via the koma 
evolution charts.

-DEMO GALLERY-
View the openings to the Worlds you visited as well as the opening and 
story cinemas. You can unlock Movies via the koma evolution charts.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
OPTIONS
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

- Stereo / Mono
(Change The sound output)

- BGM Volume 1 2 3 4 5 6

- SFX Volume 1 2 3 4 5 6

- Change the Information Character
(This lets you choose who appears on the top screen while 
you navigate through the menus.)

- Delete Game
(Throw your hard work away)


============================================================================
C2: HOW TO PLAY
============================================================================

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
IN-GAME CONTROLS
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

B = Weak Attack
Y = Strong Attack
X = Special Attack A
up + X = Special Attack B
A = Jump
down = Guard
A A = Double Jump
down + A = Get off Platform
down down = Drop faster while falling
<- <- or -> -> = Dash
<- + B/Y or -> + B/Y = Side Attack
down + B = Forced Change (Forces your opponent to change characters)
down + Y = Guard Break (Breaks the opponents guard)
down + X = Push Attack (A counter attack that pushes the opponent back)
up + B/Y = Down Attack
Select = Ultimate Action
L/R = Character Hot Keys

Use your touch screen for calling in Battle, Support characters, and 
using your Help characters.

When you are knocked off a ledge, press the B button to grab onto a near 
by ground ledge (Platforms for some reason do not count). You can hang 
from the ledge for a couple seconds then you will fall. You can either 
press left or right to come up and roll across or press up to simply 
come up without rolling. (credit to kirbycool40 for the up trick)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
THE GAUGES
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
                              ___________________
Character Nature -->  [&]----|___________________| <- J-Soul (health) Gauge
                      |       ##==============    <-- Special Gauge
Character Portrait -> |         ??????   <---- Character Name
                      \__________/

Take notice of the yellow bar. Do you see it? That's your J-Soul or Health 
bar. This dictates how much health you have, once it empties your character 
is KOed and leaves the battle. (If you've ever played a fighting game you 
should already know this)

When this happens you'll see that your KOed characters portrait is 
black and white and has a small bar in the corner of it, this bar is 
your character's health gradually returning. Once filled the character 
can be called back into the fight.

In the event all of your characters get KOed, J-Coins will scatter about 
around your characters body. After a few seconds, all of your characters 
will be revived with full health.


Next notice the small blue bar. See it? Look again... See it now? Good, 
that's your Special Gauge, this gauge is used for Special Attacks, Support 
Characters, and Help Characters. The gauge can be refilled several different 
ways:

 - Beating up your opponent
 - Collecting J-Coins (the bigger the coin, the more energy you get)
 - Collecting Pirate Heads (They restore 1 SP bar)
 - Certain Help Komas can help speed up the gauges recovery rate
 - Certain Support Komas can restore your gauges energy
 - Some Ultimate Actions can also fill the Special Gauge
 - Even some special attacks themselves can restore energy

To the left of these on the top left corner you'll see a kanji indicating 
the nature of the character. (see the Koma Nature section to learn how this 
applies to the game play)


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
BATTLE SYSTEM
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

There are three kinds of victory conditions to choose from in Battle mode, 
Versus mode, Wireless and Wifi matches.


-POINT SYSTEM-
Victories are determined by points rather than Knockouts. (of course you can 
change that in Battle Mode) How this works is, all players start with zero(0)
points. 

When you KO an enemy character, you get one(1) point while the character's 
controller loses one(1) point. 

If you KO an enemy character with your Leader character, you get two(2) 
points while the enemy character's controller loses two(2) points.

And finally, if you KO an enemy Leader character, you get two(2) points.
When your whole team is KOed, you lose an additional 2 points. 

When the timer runs out, the points are tallied and the one with the most 
points wins. It's possible to win even with zero(0) or less points. In the 
event of all players having negative number points (-1, -2, etc.) the player 
with the least amount of negative points wins.


-ELIMINATION-
Victories are determined by whose left standing. That's right, good ol'"King 
of the Hill" type rules. You'll find that this is the most popular in Wifi.
Basically you fight your opponents and KO each of their characters. 
Once you've KOed all the opponents, you win.


-J-SYMBOL-
Here everyone starts with a Star and after a few seconds another will 
drop in the middle of the stage. Your objective in this battle is to 
collect the most stars. You can get them simply by KOing and opponent 
and they will drop one of their stars, quickly run over and grab it. 
The Player with the most stars at the end wins.


In the event there is a tie, the tied players will be set into...

!!!SUDDEN DEATH!!!

They'll be dropped onto a platform with no walls and no ground. They 
will also be forced to fight with their Leader character and nothing else. 
No secondary characters, support characters or help character bonuses, 
and your SP gauge will start at zero. It's just you and your opponent(s).


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
HOT-KEY & LEADER SYSTEM
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

You can designate two characters in your Koma page as quick select 
characters via the L and R buttons. Then when you're in battle you 
can simply press L or R and get the characters you want. It's been 
shown in pervious screens that the Quick-select system is not limited 
to support characters, help and battle characters can be assigned as well.

The character you assign as Leader will be the first character you start 
with when the fight begins and the only character you'll use in Sudden Death.
If you KO your opponent with your Leader character, you get an extra win 
point. And if you KO you opponents leader with any of your characters 
(doesn’t have to be your leader), you get 2 win points. For a battle 
character to be a leader they must have a help koma placed next to them.
The character you assign as Leader will also receive a health bonus.

After creating your deck, tap either the L or R button. This will bring up
the Leader and Hot-Key options. With the cursor select the Battle character 
you wish to make Leader and assign the hot-keys to whomever you want.

You MUST assign a Leader in order for you deck to be useable in the game.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
ITEMS & STATUS EFFECTS
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

As you fight during the match, you may (or may not) notice some certain 
items seem to make the fight easier while others make it harder.
They're called "Status Effects" you'll be seeing a lot of them. 
Once one of your characters are infected with an effect, all of your 
characters will share the effect when they come onto the field. 
If however you have a character with an immunity koma attached to 
those, switching to them will remove the effect. You can only have 1 
positive and 1 negative effect on you at a time. If you get another 
effect of either kind, the previous effect is canceled.

Here is a list of the various kinds of Status Effects...

POSSITIVE STATUS EFFECTS
 - Attack-Boost = A red boxing glove with an arrow pointing up.
 (Increases you attack power)

 - Deck Power = A red boxing glove with red kana beneath it meaning "Deck".
 (Increases your attack power based on the number of characters in your deck)

 - Speed-Boost = A sneaker with an arrow pointing up.
 (Increases your movement speed)

 - Critical = A blue boxing glove with an "!".
 (Every other hit you'll deal at least double your normal attack power)

 - Toughen = A metal tile spins above your head.
 (Cuts damage you receive in half)

 - Invisibility = An invisible character above your head.
 (You become invisible to your opponents)

 - Invincibility = A star appears over your head.
 (You can no longer be harmed)

 - Regain = A blue kanji appears over your head.
 (You'll gradually regain health)

 - Infinite = A gold kanji appears over your head.
 (Your Special Gauge amount becomes infinite)

 - Null-Nature = A clear kana appears over your head.
 (Your Battle characters koma nature is removed removing your weakness to 
 koma of another nature, but also removing your strength against another nature)

 - Kinniku Power (Kinnikuman only) = The kanji for "Kinniku" appears 
   over your head.
 (Kinnikuman gains the combined effects of both Critical and Toughen)

 - Ensastu (Hiei only) = A black & white kanji appears over your head.
 (Hiei's attacks do more damage and the flames become black)

 - Mellorine (Sanji only) = Three little pink hearts appear over your head.
 (Gain an attack boost for each Female character in your deck)


NEGATIVE STATUS EFFECTS
 - Speed-Down = A sandal with an arrow pointing down.
 (Movement speed is reduced)

 - Confusion = A pair of "?" appear over your head.
 (Your left and right directions are reversed)

 - Poison = A skull appears over your head.
 (You'll slowly lose health)

 - Auto-Run = A man running appears over your head.
 (You can not stop running)

 - Paralysis = Two bolts appear over your head.
 (You lose the ability to fight and move)

 - Blindness = Either a eye with a slash though it or a DS with
   the top screen whited out appears over your head.
 (Your battle screen is blacked/whited out)

 - Shock = A word balloon with a bolt in it appears over your head.
 (You'll receive random shocks that can cancel actions and harm you)

 - Burn = A word balloon with a flame in it appears over your head.
 (You slowly lose health and you can not stop running)

 - Freeze = A word balloon with a snow flake in it appears over your head.
 (You lose the ability to fight and move)

 - Stop = A man walking with a slash through him appears over your head.
 (You can not move, turn around or jump)

 - Attack Seal = A boxing glove with a slash through it appears.
 (You can no longer attack or use Specials)

 - Guard Seal = A shield with a slash through it appears.
 (You can no longer block)

 - Battle Seal = A set of red kana with a slash through it appears.
 (You can no longer change Battle characters)

 - Support Seal = A set of blue kana with a slash through it appears.
 (You can no longer call in Support characters)

 - SP Drain = A gold kanji with an arrow pointing down appears.
 (Your SP gauge gradually drains away)

 - Judgment = A count down timer from 10-0 appears over your head.
 (When the timer reaches 0 or when you are attacked while the 
  timer is at 0, you are instantly KOed)

Some items can grant positive effects while others cast negative effects.

Here is a list of the various kinds of items that you will find...

ITEMS
 - Orange, Apple, Strawberry, Banana, Grapes, Mellon = 
 Restores a small amount of health
 - Meat = Restores a larger amount of health
 - First Aid Kit = Restores a little health to all Battle 
 characters in your deck.
 - J-Coins = Restores a small amount of SP energy
 - Bombs = Explode and damage anyone near them
 - Pirate Head = Grants 1 full SP bar
 - Red Boxing glove = Causes Attack-Boost effect
 - Blue Boxing Glove = Causes Critical effect
 - Sneaker = Causes Speed-Boost effect
 - Bubble = Causes Invisibility effect
 - Star = Causes Invincibility effect
 - Jump Magazine = Causes Infinite effect
 - Sandal = Causes Speed-Down effect
 - Mushroom = Causes Confusion effect
 - Purple Bottle = Causes Poison effect
 - Chili Pepper = Causes Auto-Run effect
 - Ink = Drops a drop of ink on everyone causing Blindness effect
 - Hammer = Drops hammers on everyone’s head causing Paralysis effect
 - Weight = Drops weights on everyone’s head causing Speed-Down effect
 - Gems = Can be used to buy more koma, and Data features
 

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
ULTIMATE ACTIONS
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

A new feature of Jump Ultimate Stars is the "Ultimate Actions". These work 
similar to a taunt, but some have special benefits such as health restoration, 
energy recovery, or even counter attacks.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
DREAM COMBO
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Useable as long as you have 2 or more Battle Characters in your deck.

For the sake of this exercise we'll use Goku, Luffy, and Ichigo as 
an example.
 
First tap on the Koma that represents your current Battle Character this 
would be the one on the battle screen now (Goku). 
Then tap your 2nd character (Luffy) and finally tap your 3rd character 
(Ichigo) you'll have to do this fast or it'll just be the same as tagging 
in and out. 

As you tap your characters you'll notice that the Battle Characters 
you've tapped are highlighted in red) then tap your first character 
you tapped before to unleash the combo. 


============================================================================
C3: ALL ABOUT KOMA
============================================================================

"Koma" the Japanese word for "Panel".

Basically a Koma is your character(s), there is no character select screen 
here rather a Koma list from which you select your character(s) and form 
your team here after referred to as a "Koma Deck". Koma are the little 
squares that make up the character portraits in the bottom screen (Here 
after referred to as "The Koma Screen"). 
The Koma Screen is a 4 x 5 grid that can hold up to 20 koma in all. 
 _ _ _ _ _
|_|_|_|_|_|
|_|_|_|_|_|
|_|_|_|_|_|
|_|_|_|_|_|

Depending how many komas a character takes up the function or attack(s) 
will change.

1 koma = Help, used for healing, boosting and other beneficial effects.

2-3 koma = Support, summons a character that attacks/status effects/etc. 
then teleports out of combat.

4-8 koma = Battle, these are the characters you control directly during 
combat. When you have more that one, you can tap on the other character 
with the stylus and tag out the characters.

Koma very in shape and size depending on the Koma itself.

Once you complete a Koma deck, it comes out looking like a manga page.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
KOMA NATURE
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

The Nature system is similar to rock, paper, scissors. Using an attack of 
an opposing nature will allow you to deal more damage than usual.

Basically, as explained in one of the tutorial stages, in the Jump World, 
all Battle and Support Characters are affiliated with an inherent nature 
either 'Laughter', 'Power', or 'Knowledge'. In the Deck Maker section 
(bottom screen) you'll see kanji symbol that designates the Koma nature.

                             __|___  
                              /   |   
                             /    /  
                             Power
                          /        \
                         /          \
                       _ 
                 _'|_ | |             ,-----
                  _|_ | |   -------   _\_|_/_
                   /  |_|              /   \
                 Knowledge            Laughter

Power beats Knowledge. Knowledge beats Laughter. And Laughter beats Power. 

When you use an attack that is strong against the opponent’s inherent 
nature, you deal 1.5 times the damage. (Luffy is Power, Bo-bobo is 
Laughter, And Yoh is Knowledge.)

Do note that some characters have special moves that are of a different 
nature than their komas inherent nature. To know which ones are like that 
see the Koma List section.

You can also recognize a characters Nature by the color around the kanji.
Red = Power
Green = Knowledge
Yellow = Laughter

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
KOMA EVOLUTION
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

A new feature of Jump Ultimate Stars is the new Koma Evolution System. 
You now need to collect different color gems that appear when you KO 
your opponent(s). 

 - Red = Power
 - Green = Knowledge
 - Yellow = Laughter
 - Blue = Friendship
 - Purple = Effort
 - White = J(ump)

Upon collecting them, you go on to add these items to your selected koma 
and evolve them into either Support or Battle komas through their 
"Evolution Chart".

    |-[?]
    |
[H]-|-[?]

...an example of a starting Evolution Chart.

    |-[S2]-[S3]
    |
[H]-|-[B4]-[B5]-[B6]

...an example of a completed Evolution Chart.

--ABRIVIATIONS--
H = Help Koma
S = Support Koma
B = Battle Koma
(The numbers indicate how many spaces the Koma takes)

You can go back anywhere in the Evolution Chart to purchase Komas you 
passed up.

Some komas will have special requirements in order for them to be unlocked.

Keep in mind that you're going to need some support komas if you want to 
have a useable deck, so don't go turning them all into battle characters.

When unlocking new koma, new paths will open up.

Example:
    |-[?]
    |
[H]-|-[B4]-[?]

Now we'll pay into the box next to B4...

    |-[?]
    |
[H]-|-[B4]-[B5]-|-[?]
                |
                |-[?]

See? This is how you unlock things in the evolution charts. Note this 
when navigating the Koma section thinking you can buy the exact koma 
you want reguardless. You can't. You'll have to pay into each piece of 
the chart. Some paths will not unlock until you pay into another character 
from the same series.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
HELP CHARACTER
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Here's how the Help koma system works. All help koma abilities are passive 
now, you have to assign them to characters via the arrows (i.e. the help koma 
with the arrow pointing at the character is the character that gets the 
effect). 

The abilities you can get from the help koma vary from triple 
jump, wall jump, air dash, health recovery, status effect immunity, 
special gauge recovery (also adds 1 to the gauge), etc.

Having two or more help komas with the same effect on one character, 
cancels out the other koma with the same effect.

Here are the following Help Koma skills that have been identified:

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
SUPPORT CHARACTER
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

A Support character comes in for an assist then leaves, but can be called 
in again. Depending on the number of panels the character takes up, the 
attack will change.

Some attacks will only take effect once the support character is hit.

Some support attacks will make the bottom screen change to look like this: 

[1][2]
[3][4]

(Depending which player you are, that slot will be a Jump logo instead.)

When you see this use your stylus and tap on the number and the 
corresponding player will be targeted and receive the attack.
Characters with this effect are listed with the term "Select a Target" 
in their effects.

Please note that all Support characters can double as Help characters.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
BATTLE CHARACTER
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Battle characters are your basic playable character. You have direct control 
over this character and depending on how much space the koma takes up the 
special attack will be different. 

Please note that all Battle characters can double as Support and/or Help 
characters.


============================================================================
C4: WALKTHROUGH
============================================================================

The new J-Universe Mode replaces J-Adventure as the new story mode. It plays 
similar to Kingdom Hearts 2 in that you are presented with a group of 
planets, from which you then get to choose which one you want to go to first.

Between worlds, press the Y button you'll be presented with 3 options;

Deck Maker (modify or create a new deck)
J-Power (evolve you komas into new battle or support characters or 
unlock stages, trivia questions, music, etc.)
Cancel (go back to J-Universe)

Some of the Stages are incomplete, and if you know what needs to be done to 
complete said stage, please email me at: ashhnt1@netscape.net. You will 
receive credit in the guide, and the thanks of all who are playing this game. 
^_^

(more info to come...)

******************************************************************************
EARTH
******************************************************************************

You'll start with Luffy, Naruto (Both playable), Goku (support), 
Muhyo and Gintoki (Both help). As you earn gems, you can evolve 
them as you see fit.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------
EARTH (TUTORIAL 1)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

1. Practice the dash (->->), then practice attacking (just beat up the box).

2. Here you have to practice you up + B/Y and down + B/Y attacks. 
Beat up the box twice to clear this stage.

3. Ok first part, practice block (hold down), next practice your down + Y 
attack, then practice your Push Attack (hold down and press X when the box 
attacks you), then stand close to the box and press down + B. Next practice 
tagging out (tap on Naruto’s koma), Next practice calling in your support 
character (tap on Goku’s koma).

4. Finally you will be given a tutorial on making a koma deck.
(Simply refer to the Deck Maker section of the guide for this info)


----------------------------------------------------------------------------
ASTAROID (TUTORIAL 2)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------

1. First attack the box, next practice a Dream Combo.

2. Do nothing, this simply explains the Nature aspects of the game.
Simply scroll through the text and you will clear it.

3. Next practice your "Punt" move. (down + Y next to a chest, crate or barrel)
Finally just fight against the box until you get a KO.

The new J-Universe Mode replaces J-Adventure as the new story mode. It plays 
similar to Kingdom Hearts 2 in that you are presented with a group of 
planets, from which you then get to choose which one you want to go to first.

Unlock: Leorio

Unlock: Biscuit

3. Complete within 3 minutes

4. Complete within 2 minutes

5. Complete within 1 minute
Unlock: Kuroro

TACTIC:
Attack the bottom 2 wall sections with Gon's standing Y attack. 
Then go to the springboard to the platform above you, and use the 
standing Y attack again (a section will be missed but don't worry 
we'll get to that). Now hop up to the 3rd platform and use the 
standing Y attack again. Now leap over to the other side and from 
the top down repeat the process. There should now be a section on 
each side untouched, switch to Killua and using the springboard 
use Killua's mid-air Y attack (you should be able to use this attack 
twice in row). Once destroyed dash to the other side and repeat.

============================================================================
C5: KOMA (CHARACTER) LIST
============================================================================

--How to Read the Koma List--

****************************************************************************
<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<series title>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
Mangaka:  <--(The author and/or artist of the manga)
Series Symbol:  <--(The symbol can be seen to the left of the koma list)
-Battle Characters-     -Support Characters-     -Help Characters-
(Characters will be listed in their corresponding sections. Please note 
that Battle characters can double as Support and Help characters and 
Support characters can double as Help characters.)
****************************************************************************

MONKEY D. LUFFY  <--(Character name)
Boost: Usopp, Shanks, Cobra(Cobra)  <--(Which characters give a boost)
Passive Effect:  <--(Special effects that the character will have regardless 
of enhancements and status effects)
Ultimate Action:  <--(Ultimate action, effect applied along side it)
Evolution Chart:  <--(Character chart to Koma unlocking)
    |-[?]
    |
[H]-|-[?]

-HELP KOMA-  <--(Koma type)
Decreases damage taken from Punches and Kicks  <--(Help Koma effect)
(Start with) <--(Where this koma can be found)

-SUPPORT KOMA-  <--(Koma type)
2 Koma: Power  <--(Koma number and attack nature)
Gomu Gomu no Hanabi  <--(Attack name, extra effects applied along side it)
[][]  <--(Koma shape)
Cost: <--(How many gems it costs)

-BATTLE KOMA-  <--(Koma type)
4 Koma: Power  <--(Koma number and nature)
J-Soul:  <--(The default amount of health the Koma has)
Gomu Rush  <--(Attack name, extra effects applied along side it)
Attack Nature: Power  <--(Nature of the attack)
[][]  <--(Koma shape)
[][]
Cost: n/a (you start with it) <--(How many gems it costs)


EVOLUTION CHART
H = Help Koma (This is where you start from)

S = Support Koma (Numbers next to these signify the number of spaces)

B = Battle Koma (Numbers next to these signify the number of spaces)

AB = Alternate Battle Koma (Same special attacks, but the character 
will be of an alternate nature. Special attacks retain their nature)

E = Evolution Path (Unlocks a new Evolution Path for another Battle 
character of the same series)

D = Data (Music, Manga Info, or World Demo. Specified in characters page)

C = Info Character (Character can be set as your menu guide via the 
main Options)

ST = Stage (New stages to battle in)

Q = Quiz (Quiz questions are added to J-Quiz)

G = Unlocks Galaxies in J-Universe mode

Passive Effects:

 - Gain SP energy when being attacked by opponents.

shoots from him. Consume 2 Colas to power up his Y attacks. Occasionally 
Franky will consume other beverages and not get the boost, this does not 
affect previous boosts. You'll know them by the different colored steams.

============================================================================
C6: SECRETS
============================================================================

- Unlock J-Quiz Mode (credit to GP03)
Clear the game once.


- Reveal all SP missions
Clear the game once.


- Unlock Champion Match Ranking in J-Arena (credit to Badtzmaru)
Clear Point, Elimination and J-Star Match Ranking in J-Arena.
After beating the first 3 ranking modes in J-Arena to get 900 
of red, green and yellow gems. Then you'll unlock champion 
ranking mode which will unlock after you beat the first three 
modes which will give you 500 of the red/green/yellow gems and 
then 200-500 of the other 3 gems. You can keep doing it over 
and over, the only drawback is that you have to fight 20 matches.


- Unlocking Pre-Made Decks
Clear all 20 matches in any of the four Ranking Modes. At the end 
you'll be challenged and if you win, you unlock a brand new deck.


- Easy Unlocking for Aisu, Rohan, Shanks & Yuria- (credit to GP03)
http://www.badongo.com/pic/367646

The Triangle unlocks Aisu, pay 400 purple gems to unlock the Taizo Mote 
King Saga world in J-Space.

The J unlocks Rohan, pay 400 purple gems to unlock the Jojo's Bizarre 
Adventure world in J-Blackhole.

The X unlocks Shanks, who is needed for Luffy's 7 and 8 komas.

The Big Dipper unlocks Yuria, who is needed for Kenshiro's 8 koma 
and Raoh's 7 & 8 komas.


- World Unlockers
Here is a list of the characters that unlock new worlds in J-Universe.

Outenkun (Houshin Engi)
Get in: One Piece Mission 1, SP
Looks: Blue skinned Goth guy
Unlocks: Houshin Engi, Death Note, Yu-Gi-Oh!

Aisu Sado (Taizo Mote King Saga)
Get in: Kinnikuman Mission 1, SP
Looks: Blue haired girl with bells in hair
Unlocks: Taizo Mote King Saga

Pochi (Ninku)
Get in: J-Space Planet X Mission 1, SP
Looks: Brown dog
Unlocks: Ninku

Ryuji Toramaru (Sakigake!! Otokojuku)
Get in: Katekyo Hitman Reborn Mission 3, SP
Looks: Guy with brown hair and whiskers
Unlocks: Jungle King Ta-chan, Sakigake!! Otokojuku

Chuubei (Midori no Makibaou)
Get in: Hokuto no Ken Mission 1, SP
Looks: Grey rat
Unlocks: Midori no Makibaou

Kyoko Inaba (Jigoku Sensei Nube)
Get in: J-Galaxy Planet X Mission 1, SP
Looks: Red haired girl with a pony-tail
Unlocks: Jigoku Sensei Nube

Papillon (Busou Renkin)
Get in: Bleach Mission 4, SP
Looks: Smiling guy with Butterfly mask
Unlocks: Busou Renkin, I"s, Ichigo 100%

Doryoku Man (Tottemo! Luckyman)
Get in: Dr. Slump Mission 2, SP
Looks: Crying guy with large eyebrows
Unlocks: Tottemo! Luckyman

Rohan Kishibe (Jojo's Bizarre Adventure)
Get in: J-Blackhole Boss Tower Mission 10, SP
Looks: Guy with black hair and a brown zigzag headband
Unlocks: Jojo's Bizarre Adventure


- Easy J-Gems Collecting (credit CinnHotPlate45)
Visit the following worlds...

Gintama planet mission 2 for Laughter(yellow) gems
Eyeshield 21 planet mission 2 for Power(red) gems
Ninku planet(only mission there) for Knowledge(green) gems

These are all "collect all the items before the time limit mission"
You have to defeat the opponents to get the gems of course but you 
don't have to clear the mission. Just kill your opponents and they 
drop their koma type gems. All the characters in the mission are of 
the type listed next to it. 
For other places to get Knowledge gems, there are other planets like 
D.Gray-man and Black Cat. Black Cat is for Knowledge & Power but it 
will take longer to get lots because all the characters aren't the 
same type, and kill your opponents, meaning the deal deathblow to them, 
with a Special Attack you get 20 gems in one chunk from them. 
Kill them naturally or with a support you get 12 in 4 piece(3 gems each) 
and they are hard to get to because they are scattered and your opponent 
is a selfish bastard that picks up stuff it doesn't need. If you are really 
good you can get nearly 400 gems a match this way, AND YOU DON'T HAVE TO 
WIN THE MATCH.


- Easy 99 hit 999 damage rating in Training mode
So you want to have 99 hits and 999 damage to show on you player profile 
and don't want any of the hard work that goes with it? Well first of all 
shame on you. And with that aside, here's the cheap and easy way to do it.

First make a deck and include 4 koma Gotenks from Dragon Ball. Now go to 
Training mode by pressing Start while in Deck making mode and simply 
spam Gotenks' X special attack over and over again.
It's that simple. :P


- Playing with your characters on-screen
Use your stylus to move your characters around, hold it down at their 
feet to make them block. Also if you press the little pirate symbol on the 
right, it will randomly scroll through all of your decks.


============================================================================
C7: PRE-MADE DECKS
============================================================================

Pre-Made Decks are decks that are made by the developers for you the gamers.
You can unlock them in J-Universe and the Ranking modes.

In the Ranking modes, you have to clear all 20 matches in and of the four 
rankings. At the end you'll be challenged and if you win, you unlock a 
brand new deck.

window.onhashchange = hashHasChanged;

function hashHasChanged()
{
	$(document).scrollTop(window.pageYOffset-130);
}

$().ready(function()
{
	$('.faq_bookmark_jumper').clone().appendTo('#faq_header_wrap');
	if(window.pageYOffset>=150)
	{
		$('#faq_header_wrap').addClass('fixed');
	}

	window.onscroll = function()
	{
		if(window.pageYOffset>=150)
		{
			$('#faq_header_wrap').addClass('fixed');
			$('.top_link').addClass('fixed').css('display','block');
		}
		else
		{
			$('#faq_header_wrap').removeClass('fixed');
			$('.top_link').removeClass('fixed').css('display','none');
		}
	};

	
	$.each($('.ffaq img.imgnofloat'),function(){
		var image = $(this);
		if(image.attr('width')>400)
		{
			image.addClass('bigresize');
		}
	});

	
	var autobm = window.setInterval(function()
	{
		if(window.pos==$(document).scrollTop() || window.autobmstop==true)
			clearInterval(autobm);
		else
			set_bookmark(1);
	}, 5000);
});

$('img.imgresize').bind('click touch', function()
{
	if($(this).css('max-width')=='none')
		$(this).css({ 'width':'100%','max-width':'100%' });
	else
		$(this).css({ 'width':'auto','max-width':'none' });
});
var timer, lastsearch;

$('body :not(#faq_search_results)').bind('click touch', function(e)
{
	if($('#faq_search_results').css('display')=='block')
	{
		e.stopPropagation();
		if($(this).attr('id')!='results_table' && $(this).attr('id')!='faq_search' && $(this).attr('id')!='faq_search_input' && $(this).attr('class')!='head' && $(this).attr('id')!='home_links')
		{
			dismiss_faq_search_results(0);
		}
	}
});

$('#faq_search_input, .search_mobile').click(function(e)
{
	e.preventDefault();
	if($(this).attr('class')=='search_mobile')
	{
		$('#faq_search_module').toggle();
	}
	$('#faq_search_module, #faq_search_results').addClass('fixed');
	$('#faq_header_wrap').addClass('search');
		if($('#faq_search_results').css('display')!='block')
	{
		$('#faq_search_results').show();
	}
	if($('#results_table tr').length==0)
		$('.close_results').hide();
	$('#faq_search_input').focus();
});

$('#faq_search_input').keyup(function()
{
	if(lastsearch != $('#faq_search_input').val())
	{
		if(timer)
			clearTimeout(timer);
		lastsearch = $('#faq_search_input').val();
		if($('#faq_search_input').val().length>=3)
		{
			$('#faq_search_results').show();
			timer = setTimeout(function(){ faq_search(45856, $('#faq_search_input').val()) },150);
		}
		else
		{
			dismiss_faq_search_results(1);
		}
	}
});

function dismiss_faq_search_results(fixed)
{
	$('#faq_search_results').hide();
	$('#search_overlay').fadeTo(200,0).remove();
	if(!fixed)
	{
		$('#faq_search_module, #faq_search_results').removeClass('fixed');
		$('#faq_header_wrap').removeClass('search');
		if($('.search_mobile').is(':visible'))
			$('#faq_search_module').toggle();
	}
	$('#results_table').empty();
}

function search_result(row)
{
	if($('#results_table tr[data-row="'+row+'"]').length>0)
	{
		var url = $('#results_table tr[data-row="'+row+'"]').attr('data-url');
		if(url.indexOf('search_highlight')>=0)
			eval(url);
		else
			window.location = $('#results_table tr[data-row="'+row+'"]').attr('data-url');
	}
}

$('body :not(#faq_toc_header *, #faq_toc a.toc_toggle *)').bind('click touch', function(e)
{
	if($('#faq_toc_header').css('display')=='block')
	{
		e.stopPropagation();
		if($(this).attr('class')!='toc_toggle active' && $(this).attr('id')!='faq_toc_header')
		{
			$('.toc_toggle').removeClass('active');
			$('#faq_toc_header').css('display','none');
		}
	}
});

function toc_toggle()
{
	if($('.toc_toggle').hasClass('active'))
	{
		$('a.toc_toggle').removeClass('active');
		$('#faq_toc_header').css('display','none');
	}
	else
	{
		$('a.toc_toggle').addClass('active');
		$('#faq_toc_header').css('display','block');
	}
}

function faq_search(id, term)
{
	var term = $('#faq_search_input').val();
	var len = term.length;
	if(term.length<3)
		return;

	$.ajax(
	{
		type: 'POST',
		url: '/ajax/faq_search',
		data:  { id: 45856, term: term, key: 'ad5426a9' },
		success: function(response)
		{
			if(response[0]=='error')
			{
				$('#results_table').html('No Results Found - Close');
				$('.close_results').remove();
				$('#faq_search_results').show();
			}
			else
			{
				window.currentpage = 0;
				$('#results_table').html('');
				$('.close_results').remove();

				$.each(response,function(j,i)
				{
					var val = i.value;
					val = val.split('-');
					var page = val[0];
										if(window.currentpage!=page)
					{
						window.currentpage = page;
					}
					var link, pagenum = '';

					
										{
						link = "search_highlight(45856,"+val[0]+",2,"+val[1]+","+len+");";
					}
					
					$('#results_table').append(''+i.label+''+pagenum+'');
				});

				$('#results_table').after('Close Results');
			}
		}
	});
}

function search_highlight(file_id,page,format,pos,len,section)
{
	window.textpage = page+1;
	$.ajax(
	{
		type: 'POST',
		url: '/ajax/faq_search_highlight',
		data: { id: file_id, page: page, for: format, pos: pos, len: len, section: '', key: 'ad5426a9' },
		success: function(response)
		{
					$('span[name="search_jump"]').remove();
			$("span.faq_highlight").contents().unwrap();
			$('pre#faqspan-'+window.textpage).html(response);
				$.each($('.ffaq img.imgnofloat'),function(){
			var image = $(this);
			if(image.attr('width')>400)
			{
				image.addClass('bigresize');
			}
		});
		var offset = $('span[name="search_jump"]').offset().top - 100;
		if(offset<100)
			$(document).scrollTop(0);
		else
			$(document).scrollTop(offset);
		}
	});
}


function set_bookmark(auto)
{
	var pos = $(document).scrollTop();
	if(pos!=window.pos)
		window.pos = pos;
	else
		return;
	var page = 0;
	if(page!=window.page)
		window.page = page;
	if(auto)
		auto = 1;
	$.ajax(
	{
		type: 'POST',
		url: '/ajax/faq_bookmark',
		data: { id: 45856, pos: pos, page: page, section: '', auto: auto, key: 'ad5426a9' },
		success: function(response)
		{
			if(response)
			{
				if(response=="Missing cookie.")
					window.autobmstop = true;
				$('.faq_bookmark_jump').fadeOut();
				$('#faq_bookmark').addClass('active');
				var html = $('.bm_jumper_inner').html();
				$('.bm_jumper_inner').animate({ 'opacity':0 },200,function()
				{
					if($('.faq_bookmark_jumper:not(.active)'))
						$('.faq_bookmark_jumper').css('visibility','visible');
					$(this).html('Bookmark Saved').animate({ 'opacity':1 },200);
					$('.faq_bookmark_jumper').animate({ 'opacity':1 },200);
				});
				setTimeout(function()
				{
					if($('.faq_bookmark_jumper:not(.active)'))
					{
						$('.faq_bookmark_jumper').animate({ 'opacity':0 },200,function()
						{
							$(this).css('visibility','hidden');
						});
					}
					$('.bm_jumper_inner').animate({ 'opacity':0 },200,function()
					{
						$(this).html($.trim(html)).animate({ 'opacity':1 },200);
					});
				}, 3000);
			}
		}
	});
}

function bookmark_jump_dismiss()
{
	$('#faq_header > .faq_bookmark_jumper').css('visibility','hidden');
	$('#faq_header_wrap > .faq_bookmark_jumper').remove();
	$('#faq_bookmark').removeClass('active');
}


function page_top()
{
	$(window).scrollTop(0);
}

function make_printable()
{
	var faqdiv = $('#faqtext').detach();
	faqdiv.appendTo('#fullscreen_overlay');
	$('#fullscreen_overlay').show();
	$('.wrapper').hide();
	$('#faqtext').addClass("fullscreen_takeover");
	$('#faqtext').append('Restore Page');
	$(window).scrollTop(0);
}

function restore_page()
{
	$('#restore_page').remove();
	$('#faqtext').removeClass("fullscreen_takeover");
	$('#fullscreen_overlay').hide();
	$('.wrapper').show();
	var faqdiv = $('#faqtext').detach();
	faqdiv.appendTo('#faqwrap');
	$(window).scrollTop(0);
}



function set_rec_val(val)
{
	
	var ftype = "guide";	
	$('#recval').val(val);
	if(val==2)
		$('.rec_comment').attr('placeholder','What is missing from this '+ftype+'? (140 character max)');
	$('#rec_one').hide();
	$('#rec_two').show();
	return;
}

function hide_rec()
{
	$('#rec_two').hide();
	$('#rec_three').show();
	var timer = setTimeout(function()
	{
		$('#contrib_rec').fadeOut('slow').remove();
		$('#faq_header_wrap').addClass('rec_closed');
	}, 2000);
}

function rec_submit(item_id, item_type_id, button)
{
	var recval = $('#recval').val();
	if($('.rec_comment'))
		var reccommentval = $('.rec_comment').val();
	else
		var reccommentval = '';

	$.ajax(
	{
		type: 'POST',
		url: '/ajax/gamespace_contrib_rec',
		data: { item: item_id, type: item_type_id, btn: button, val: recval, comment: reccommentval, key: 'ad5426a9' },
		success: function(response)
		{
			if(response==2)
			{
				track_event(79);
				return;
			}
			else
				hide_rec();
		}
	});
}
